- Tool definitions schema token budget bumped from 38,500 to 39,000 in `ToolSchemaSizeTests.cs` to accommodate typed Selection filter authoring in `genexus_workwith` ([#559](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/559)), attribute typing (`dataType`, `length`, `decimals`, `basedOn`) in `genexus_create` ([#541](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/541)), and `<tabs>`/`<textBlock>`/userAction container parameters in `genexus_wwp` ([#551](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/551), [#552](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/552)). Measured ~38,783 tokens (~217 headroom).

## v3.15.0 - 2026-10-13


### Tracked issues

- [#495](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/495) — [Bug] genexus_edit síncrono em part=Events: gravação persistida reportada como WriteVerificationUnavailable (readFailure), de forma intermitente, sem o motivo da falha de leitura
- [#496](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/496) — [Docs/DX] genexus_edit part=Events: o modo estrito recusa por falta de isolamento o object-level save que o patch comum faz sem aviso; a descrição de requireObjectSave reforça a confusão


### Added

- The independent Rust `gx-engine` now compiles `And`, `Or`, and `Not` with logical precedence and short-circuit evaluation. Authored differential fixtures matched 91 cases/92 values on the official GeneXus 18 .NET path and the Rust path in KBTeste; malformed/oversized conditions and helper evaluation have regression coverage. Complete SDK replacement remains the objective, not a current compatibility claim.

- `GXMCP_GXWIRE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` sets the wall-clock budget for one `gxwire.exe` invocation, overriding the per-action defaults.

### Changed

- CI runs its independent lanes in parallel instead of as one 26-step sequential job, and caches .NET build outputs. Every lane and every check it ran before still runs; only the ordering changed. Measured on the pre-change workflow: 813 s for `test`, of which 390 s was the two .NET test projects run one after the other even though neither depends on the other. `dotnet` is now a two-leg matrix (`gateway`, `worker`) on separate runners, alongside `toolchain` (CLI + Nexus IDE), `rust` (gxwire) and `contracts` (Python + docs). `test` is deliberately kept as the job name because branch protection requires a check context called exactly `test`; it is now the gate that depends on all four lanes and asserts the one result no single lane can judge - the combined coverage threshold over both projects. `scripts/coverage/collect.ps1` gained `-Label` for the matrix legs; with no label it still runs both in sequence, and a labelled leg now fails loudly instead of writing a skip marker that a CI leg would silently turn into a missing report.

### Internal

- **GxWire reindexes incrementally instead of reparsing the whole Knowledge Base on every edit.** `genexus_wire` is mandatory architectural discovery, and an agent session edits one object at a time; until now any change to any file made the next `genexus_wire` call re-read and re-lex every source in the Knowledge Base. The index now stores each file's parse output alongside the graph and reuses it while that file's `(mtime, size)` is unchanged, so only the objects that actually moved are reparsed. Measured in-process on a 4,000-object Knowledge Base (median of 5, release profile): one-object edit **769 ms → 201 ms (3.8x)**, object added **797 ms → 199 ms**, object deleted **759 ms → 201 ms**, unchanged load **143 ms → 89 ms**, index write **95 ms → 18 ms**. Through the real `gxwire.exe` CLI on the same Knowledge Base a one-object edit went from 440 ms to 156 ms (2.8x) with identical caller lists. Costs: the index file grows from 5.0 MB to 9.3 MB (the graph is now stored together with the per-file parse entries it was built from), and a full cold parse is about 15% slower because it materializes every file's parse result before assembling the graph. Through the CLI on a freshly copied Knowledge Base the cold build is indistinguishable (10.72 s vs 10.76 s).

- GxWire index detection now keys each file on `(mtime, size)` rather than `mtime` alone, so a same-millisecond rewrite that changes length cannot pass as fresh, and the freshness probe is a single directory walk with one stat per file instead of two walks.

- GxWire directory indexing reads file type from the walk instead of calling `path.is_file()`, removing one stat syscall per Knowledge Base file on every path.

- CI validates PR updates once instead of duplicating branch-push runs, cancels superseded PR runs, and caches npm, NuGet, and toolchain-keyed gxwire build dependencies. The plugin scanner still runs on main pushes; all validation steps and required check names remain unchanged.

- GxWire no longer infers an object kind from a single leading letter. A name-based guess could not tell a Procedure from a Transaction named after a person, so `PessoaDadosFiscaisAdicionais` was reported as `kind: "Proc"` and `callers` answered about the wrong object type; unambiguous two-letter prefixes are now checked first and an ambiguous name reports `Unknown` rather than guessing wrong ([#500](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/500)). Content detection remains authoritative for XML exports, where the element name states the real kind.

- `genexus_wire impact` now reports Procedures reached through an attribute formula, which it previously omitted entirely ([#501](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/501)). A Procedure called from `[Formula: ...]` affects every object reading that attribute, but `formulas` came back empty because the lexer had no rule for a formula at all. Formula bodies are parsed across physical lines (GeneXus allows them to wrap) and nested Procedure calls are all reported, while built-ins and plain attribute references inside the formula are excluded so the guard does not invent relationships that would overstate a blast radius.

- `TextPersistenceVerifier` canonicalizes line endings for every part under `exact`, not only for the code parts. A Structure is authored by one layer and re-read by another, so a CRLF request against an LF post-save read differed on line endings alone. Line endings carry no content in any part, so this cannot hide a real difference, and a guard pins that a genuine content difference still fails.

### Fixed

- Three ways `genexus_wire` could report a confident wrong answer, each measured against a live installation before the fix. All three fail in the same direction: the graph looks populated and is wrong, which is the dangerous shape for a tool whose `safe_delete` and `impact` reason over it.

  - **A same-length edit within the same millisecond was invisible to the cache.** The freshness signature truncated the timestamp with `as_millis()`, discarding resolution the filesystem already had - NTFS records 100-nanosecond ticks, and two writes inside one millisecond produced an identical signature whenever the length matched. `Call(Alpha)` becoming `Call(Bravo)` is the same byte length; the graph kept answering `count: 1` for the removed symbol while the new one was `SymbolNotFound`. Length cannot close this alone and neither can milliseconds, so the full timestamp is used now (`FileParse::mtime_ms` became `mtime_ticks`, and `CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION` moved to 4 so a cache written in milliseconds is rebuilt rather than misread). The window narrows from 1 ms to 100 ns. It does not close completely: a tool that restores the previous timestamp exactly is still undetectable, which is the deliberate price of not hashing content on every query.

  - **A source file that is not valid UTF-8 lost every reference it had.** `read_to_string` rejects the whole file, and the object fell back to an empty parse - it still appeared as a node, so nothing looked wrong, while every call in it vanished from the graph. A Windows-1252 description is enough to trigger it, which is not exotic for a Brazilian Knowledge Base. Files are now decoded lossily; call syntax is ASCII, so the edges survive and only characters that were already unreadable are replaced.

  - **GeneXus metadata at the Knowledge Base root was indexed as objects.** `.xml` is both a GeneXus object export and where the IDE keeps its own bookkeeping, and the two are indistinguishable by extension or file name. `C:\KBs\KBTeste` indexed to 30 nodes built from `CategoryViewConfig`, `iar_objs` and `nav_objs`, reported as hubs with `kind: "Proc"` and `kind: "Tbl"` - the IDE's own files answering as if they were architecture. Those three stems are now skipped, but only at the analysed root, which is the only place that metadata lives: an export that nests its objects is unaffected, and a deeper object sharing one of the names is still indexed. A blocklist rather than a content classifier on purpose - telling an exported object from metadata by content needs the real export schema, and guessing at one would exclude genuine objects.


- An attribute description GeneXus generated for itself no longer fails a write verdict ([#494](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/494)). `genexus_structure add_attribute` persisted the attribute and the receipt still reported `WriteNotPersisted` with `partialPersistenceDetected: true`, because the SDK materializes an attribute together with a description derived from its own name that the request never mentioned. The description is dropped only when it matches what the SDK would derive - "generated" cannot be told from "caller-supplied" any other way, and treating every description as generated would pass a write that wrote the wrong description. Only for a Structure part, and a formula or `[Nullable]` after the description is preserved verbatim, since that is what the caller wrote. Containment was deliberately not used: "persisted contains requested" proves an addition but not a removal, so a patch that failed to delete a line would read as persisted. Partially resolves the issue: the remaining cause is that the SDK does not honour the requested insertion point (`after`), which is a separate defect in the Structure apply path.

- GxWire index files now carry an explicit format marker, so an index left behind by a different gxwire build is discarded rather than decoded as if it were ours (which could read arbitrary bytes as collection lengths and try to reserve gigabytes). An index that cannot be decoded - truncated, empty, or garbage - is now rebuilt from disk *and re-saved*; previously it failed to decode and every later call reparsed the whole Knowledge Base without ever refreshing the index.

- **Build Watchdog and Termination Diagnostics**: Clarified failure origin (`failureOrigin: "watchdog"` / `"watchdog_with_errors"` / `"errors"` / `"process_exit"`), made `ExitCode` nullable (`null` instead of misleading `0` when child process exit code was not obtained), reported child process state (`childProcessState: "terminated" | "exited" | "running" | "unknown" | "none"`), tracked confirmed stages (`confirmedStages`), and ensured build logs are flushed immediately to disk (`fullLogPath`) on watchdog termination so consumers can reconcile results without re-running Build All ([#475](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/475)).

- Reconciled `genexus_workwith` contract and verification: removed inaccurate optimistic concurrency claim from schema and documented target-scoped locking; made `dryRun` plan declare performed validations along with explicit limits on procedure parameter arity/types and expression syntax/semantics (with advisory signature arity inspection when available); and made post-save verification compare all promised fields (name, caption, procedure, condition, ordered parameters) against reread instance state rather than echoing request parameters ([#476](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/476)).

- **WorkWith pattern default materialization investigation and receipt contract** ([#477](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/477)):

  - Investigated and documented GeneXus Pattern SDK default materialization (`page="<default>"` → `10` and default action expansion) occurring during standard WorkWith instance serialization (`docs/workwith-default-materialization.md`).

  - `WorkWithSdkAdapter.AddAction` now exposes `route` (`native-sdk` | `xml-fallback`), `verificationScope`, and `defaultNormalization` in both `dryRun` plan and write receipts.

  - Hardened post-write verification to fail closed if neighbor tabs are truncated after save (`verifyTabs.Count >= tabs.Count`).

  - `TryAddActionToXml` now outputs with `SaveOptions.DisableFormatting`, preserving unmutated elements, whitespace, and literal `<default>` attributes without reformatting.

  - Added unit test fixtures in `WorkWithServiceTests` verifying defaults preservation on Selection and neighbor tabs across XML fallback authoring.

- Qualified `routeUnsupported` diagnostic finding remediation and `PatternRouteUnsupported` rejection hint to distinguish reapply refusal from property editing: reapply remains unsupported on the generic route, existing property edits are permitted but whether derived objects update is not measured by the edit call and recorded results vary by environment, and callers are advised to apply the pattern in the GeneXus IDE if derived objects do not follow ([#478](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/478)).

- Preserved sanitized diagnostic records and failing stage attribution (`resolve_object`, `refresh_object`, `access_part`, `query_variables`, `extract_content`) across the object read path instead of swallowing exceptions or degrading to generic `LegacyWorkerError`, exposing ambient correlation ID, UTC timestamps, execution duration, bounded stack traces, and non-blocking KB state snapshots without leaking sensitive credentials or variable values ([#479](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/479)).

- Updated tool profile counts across documentation (`docs/llm_cli_mcp_playbook.md` and `docs/environment_variables.md`) to match `ToolProfileFilter` allowlists and canonical `tool_definitions.json` (standard 20, core 15, authoring 35, devops 37, ui 23, db 19, all 60), included `standard` in the `GXMCP_PROFILE` enumeration, and introduced regression guard tests in `ToolProfileTests` to prevent doc/code drift ([#480](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/480)).

- Salvamento isolado de Events: materializa a fonte antes da serialização nativa do snapshot para comparar Rules e demais partes pela mesma representação canônica, preservando a representação de restauração e a recusa de alterações reais. A prévia valida o snapshot usado pelo Save; o recibo distingue persistência transitória, transação confirmada e operação idempotente e informa o custo de inventário e Save. ([#495](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/495)).

- Eventos: `baseVersion` e `expectedVersion` atendem à mesma barreira de concorrência em patches; valores divergentes são recusados antes do despacho. A prévia informa a capacidade de persistência isolada e as recusas preservam os pins efetivos, a revisão do perfil e as evidências de rollback. ([#496](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/496)).

- Salvamento isolado de Events: corrige a seleção da sobrecarga de comparação para preservar todas as partes irmãs, excluindo somente Events. Adiciona o conjunto coerente do SDK U16 `18.0.16.189595`, mantendo a recusa de builds misturados ou desconhecidos e a verificação transacional de Save, releitura e rollback.

- `genexus_search_source` no longer drops distinct lines that happen to have byte-identical text in the same part of the same object: the dedup key read `hit["line"]`, but source hits publish `lineNumber`, so the position component was always empty and every repeated line collapsed into a single hit while the envelope still declared `partial: false` and `truncated: false`, making occurrence counts across an edit silently wrong ([#497](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/497)). Key construction is now a single `HitDedupKey` helper, which also removes the duplicated O(n²) key rebuild.

- Nexus IDE packaging no longer breaks on dependency bumps that CI could not see: `@types/vscode` was raised to `^1.140.0` while `engines.vscode` stayed at `^1.93.0`, and `@vscode/vsce` only rejects that combination at package time, so `vsce package` failed while compile, lint and tests stayed green ([#482](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/482)). `@types/vscode` is pinned to `~1.93.0` - the extension uses no VS Code API newer than 1.93, verified by compiling against the older types (a post-1.93 API such as `ChatResponseThinkingPart` correctly fails with TS2339) - and CI now runs `npm run package` and asserts exactly one `.vsix` matching `package.json`, so this class of breakage fails the build instead of the release.

- `genexus_workwith add_action` no longer claims a preservation it does not perform. The `defaultNormalization` disclosure in both the dryRun plan and the write receipt asserted "functional behavior and pattern bindings are preserved" after the native SDK materialized `page="<default>"` into a concrete value - but GeneXus then treats that attribute as an explicit instance-level override, so it stops following a later change to the pattern template default. The instance does stay functional and its identity, tabs and actions are intact; what it loses is the dynamic binding, and an agent reading the old text reasoned about future default changes from a false premise ([#477](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/477)). The disclosure is now a named constant shared by both routes, `docs/workwith-default-materialization.md` states the override consequence instead of answering "Yes", and its acceptance checklist records the one case that was answered by inference rather than measurement (a later template default change in an isolated fixture, which needs a live Knowledge Base with an authored pattern).

- `genexus_wire` no longer leaves an ownerless `gxwire.exe` behind and no longer deadlocks on a chatty child. Three defects in the invocation path, reported against 3.14.0 on a 15,170-file export ([#498](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/498)):

  - **Orphaned process.** The 30 s budget expired, the gateway returned `GxwireException`, and the child kept indexing for roughly two more minutes with nobody holding it. The process tree is now killed before the timeout is reported, and the caller's `finally` kills it again for cancellations that arrive from outside.

  - **Pipe deadlock.** stdout was read to end before stderr was touched. A child that fills the stderr buffer blocks writing while the gateway blocks reading stdout, so both wait until the budget expires. gxwire is chatty on stderr, so this was the normal case. Both pipes are now drained concurrently.

  - **`action=index` always failed.** Index is the only action that parses the whole Knowledge Base and takes minutes, yet it shared the 30 s query budget. It now gets 900 s, and an expired budget returns a distinct `GxwireTimeout` that names the budget and points at `GXMCP_GXWIRE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS`, instead of a generic exception.

- `genexus_wire callers`/`impact` now find the call forms that were invisible in the graph, and stop misreporting one of them ([#500](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/500)). Measured on a 15,170-file GeneXus 18 XML export before the fix: a Procedure invoked as a function had 36 textual occurrences resolving to a single caller, `.Popup(` returned nothing at all, and `&x = procName(...)` was recorded as an attribute read.

  - Procedure-as-function calls (`procName(args)`) are now parsed. The rule is deliberately narrow: a bare `identifier(` is a call only when the identifier is not a control word or built-in, because matching everything would invent edges to phantom nodes for every built-in and inflate the blast radius of anything attached to them. A guard covers control words, casts and built-ins, and another confirms a genuine call nested inside a built-in's argument list is still found.

  - `.Popup(` is recognized alongside `.Call(`; a Transaction opened in a popup used to answer `empty: true`, which is presented as a definitive empty rather than a miss.

  - `&x = procName(...)` is a call, not an attribute read. It was classified `reads_att`, which made a Transaction look like an attribute consumer while the actual call edge went missing.

  - Genuine attribute reads on the same line shape (`&Total = CustomerBalance`) are unaffected.

- Transaction Structure authoring by `genexus_structure` now handles a multiline attribute formula, which previously failed three different ways and could leave a stray global Attribute behind ([#492](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/492)). `mode=ops` serializes the whole Structure and parses it back line by line, but GeneXus allows a formula to wrap across physical lines, so each continuation arrived as its own node: the truncated first line failed formula validation, or the continuation was read as a level name (`Caracteres inválidos para Nome do nível`), or it was created as a separate attribute. Wrapped declarations are now rejoined before parsing, so an attribute entry stays atomic and insertion lands after the complete entry. Brackets inside a quoted description are not counted, and an unterminated formula is still emitted so the caller reports the real syntax error instead of the text silently disappearing.

- A Transaction Structure write that fails no longer leaves an orphaned global Attribute in the Knowledge Base ([#492](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/492)). A new attribute needs its global `Attribute` saved before the Transaction can reference it, but that save is outside the Transaction's own transaction; when the Transaction was then rejected the global survived, so a failed write reported failure while still leaving a stray object in the KB. Globals created during a parse are now recorded and removed when that parse does not complete. Removal is best effort and never masks the original failure.

- `genexus_analyze mode=callers` again resolves line-level call sites instead of reporting `CallerSitesUnconfirmed` with `callSiteCount: 0` for every target ([#491](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/491), follow-up to [#287](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/287)). The cross-check that recovers call sites from the live SDK reference graph reads each caller's parts and scans them for call syntax, but it was handed a JSON read envelope instead of the part text - and that envelope wraps a **base64** body, because `ProcessTextResponse` returns plain text only for `client: "mcp"` while the scan reads with the default `client: "ide"`. The scan therefore searched JSON punctuation and base64 for call syntax, found none, and reported a confident-looking zero while `sdkCallers` was fully populated. Both layers are now stripped before the scan; a payload with nothing scannable is reported as such instead of being scanned as noise. The failure was silent because a base64 envelope is not an error envelope - it parses, and the previous guard only skipped payloads containing an `"error"` key. Verified live on GeneXus 18.0.10: `callers of PCalculaDesconto` went from `callSiteCount: 0` / `CallerSitesUnconfirmed` to `callSiteCount: 2` with `provenance: sdk-reference-cross-check` and the exact call line.

- `genexus_wire` no longer writes into the directory it analyses, and no longer answers from a Knowledge Base it cannot read ([#499](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/499)). The tool published itself as read-only (`readOnlyHint: true`, "Mutating actions: none") while doing two things that contract does not admit. **It wrote its own index into the analysed folder** - `.gxwire_cache.bin`, which with `kb=` lands inside the Knowledge Base. The index now lives under `%LOCALAPPDATA%\GxMcp\Cache\gxwire`, keyed by a hash of the resolved path so two Knowledge Bases never share one. gxwire gained `--cache-path`; without it the historical in-tree path is unchanged, so invoking gxwire directly on a text tree behaves as before. **It also answered from a native Knowledge Base**: a native GeneXus 18 KB keeps its object sources in the KB database, so walking its folder indexes generated output and reports it as a graph - measured live on `C:\KBs\KBTeste`, which produced 30 nodes classified from `CategoryViewConfig`, `iar_objs` and `nav_objs` with zero edges. Such a KB is now refused with `GXWIRE_REQUIRES_TEXT_SOURCE_TREE`, the evidence that identified it, and the SDK-backed tools to use instead. Detection is two-tiered on purpose: the KB database settles it outright, while `GXLOCK`/`.gxw` count only when there is no source text at the root. A KB root carries GeneXus metadata XML (`CategoryViewConfig.xml`, `iar_objs.xml`, `nav_objs.xml`) that matches the indexed extensions, so a single-tier rule lets the real case through; refusing on any marker at all would break the XML/text export that works today.

### Internal

- GxWire gained `build_index`/`save_index`, which carry the per-file parse entries, and `save_cache` (graph without reuse state) was removed: persisting a graph alone guarantees the next load reparses everything, so the API no longer offers a footgun. `genexus_wire --index` records the parse entries, so the first incremental reindex after a full build is already cheap.

- Added `incremental_reindex_bench.rs` (end-to-end reindex timings, median of 5; sized down in debug builds so CI does not pay for numbers it cannot read) and `incremental_reindex_tests.rs`, whose guards compare an incrementally maintained graph against a cold rebuild of a pristine copy across edit, add, delete, rename, same-size rewrite, unreadable index, and empty Knowledge Base, and which assert that an unchanged Knowledge Base does not rewrite its index (the only externally visible symptom of the write and read layouts drifting apart).

## v3.14.0 - 2026-10-11


### Tracked issues

- [#450](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/450) — [Melhoria] Adicionar índice de docs/ (ou referenciar os docs de capacidade a partir do README/AGENTS)
- [#452](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/452) — [Investigação][Follow-up #353] Save do WorkWith padrão persiste a instância, mas não propaga a descrição ao painel no GX18 U15
- [#453](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/453) — [Docs/DX][Follow-up #352] Pontos residuais: exemplo de ordenação, orientação de regeneração e recibo de PatternInstance
- [#454](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/454) — [Bug][Follow-up #453/#260] Diagnóstico encaminha WorkWith padrão a WWP; listagem aceita alvo incompatível
- [#455](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/455) — [Feature] Autoria tipada de actions no General do WorkWith padrão


### Added

- **GxWire: Native Rust Architectural Discovery, Call Graph & Impact Analysis Engine (Headline Feature)**:
  - High-performance, zero-allocation native Rust engine (`publish/gxwire.exe`, 1.12 MB) purpose-built for AI coding agents to navigate large GeneXus Knowledge Bases in sub-10ms without consuming excessive context tokens or serializing on the COM SDK thread.
  - **Core Capabilities for AI Agents & Developers:**
    - **Context-Budgeted Task Packing (`action=pack_task`)**: Proactively discovers symbols, dependencies, and blast radius for a given task, ranking relevant objects and trimming payloads to fit model token budgets.
    - **Shortest-Path BFS Call Tracing (`action=path` / `--from` and `--to`)**: Traces forward call chains between any two objects across Compressed Sparse Row (CSR) indices, reconstructing intermediate hops with exact source line numbers.
    - **Natural Language Search with Domain Synonym Expansion (`action=for`)**: Expands conversational intents and standard Portuguese/GeneXus business abbreviations (`fat` → Faturamento, `ped` → Pedido, `cli` → Cliente, `forn` → Fornecedor, `nf` → NotaFiscal, `tit` → Titulo, `rec` → Receber, `pag` → Pagar, `prc` → Procedure, `trn` → Transaction, `wp` → WebPanel, `prod` → Produto, `est` → Estoque, etc.).
    - **Data Dependency & Attribute Extraction**: Extracts `ReadsAttr` relationships from `Where Attr = &Var` clauses and assignments (`&Var = Attr`), indexing tables, transactions, web panels, SDPanels, and procedures.
    - **Incremental Freshness Cache (`.gxwire_cache.bin`)**: High-speed binary disk cache with millisecond-precision file modification timestamp (`mtime`) checks, detecting edits automatically without manual re-indexing.
    - **Visual Architecture Flowcharts (`--format mermaid`)**: Generates render-ready `graph LR` / `graph TD` diagrams for paths, callers, impact trees, and architectural hubs, rendering directly in Markdown artifacts.
    - **Safe Deletion & Blast Radius Analysis (`action=safe_delete` / `action=impact`)**: Evaluates transitive callers and dependencies to verify if an object can be safely removed or refactored without breaking downstream consumers.
    - **Full Compliance with the 10 AXI (Agent eXperience Interface) Principles**:
      - Native **TOON (Tabular Object-Oriented Notation)** format (`--format toon`) yielding ~40% token savings over JSON.
      - Content-First execution (running with no arguments or `action=status` returns live KB health and PageRank hubs without diagnostic friction).
      - Budget-aware truncation hints (`--full` flag available for exhaustive listings).
      - Pre-computed decision metrics (`can_delete`, `blast_radius`, `est_tokens`).
      - Definitive empty states (`empty="true"` / `empty: true`) preventing agent retry loops.
      - Next-step disclosures on every output guiding subsequent MCP calls.
  - **Surface & Transport Availability**:
    - First-class Gateway MCP tool `genexus_wire` (actions: `pack_task`, `status`, `for`, `callers`, `impact`, `path`, `slice`, `safe_delete`, `index`, `doctor`) with cross-tool discovery guidance in `genexus_read` and `genexus_query`.
    - Integrated CLI forwarder via `genexus-mcp wire` and standalone binary launcher `gxwire`.
    - Comprehensive test suite (24 tests) validating 50,000-node scale benchmarks, cyclic call handling, token pruning, and GeneXus 18 live KB parity.

- The Gateway can now ask the human directly through MCP elicitation (`elicitation/create`, MCP 2025-06-18). When a KB-bound call fails with `KB_AMBIGUOUS` or `KB_CONTEXT_REQUIRED`, clients that support elicitation show a KB picker; the choice is selected for the session and the call is replayed. Irreversible calls made without `confirm=true` (`genexus_delete_object`, `genexus_data_view action=delete`, `genexus_transfer action=import dryRun=false`, `genexus_deploy action=deploy`) ask the human for approval instead of failing, and a refusal returns `HUMAN_DECLINED` without reaching the Worker. `GXMCP_ELICITATION=strict` also asks when the agent passed `confirm=true`; `off` disables it. Clients without the capability are unaffected. See `docs/elicitation.md`.

- Agents can now choose the right tool before reading any resource. `tools/list` published every tool's description as only `Full guidance: genexus://kb/tool-help/<name>`, so MCP clients that do not read resources up front (most of them) picked among 58 tools by name alone. Each published description is now a sentence-aware purpose summary of the source description, bounded by `ToolProfileFilter.MaxToolSummaryLength` (200 characters), followed by the same tool-help pointer; the complete description and examples stay in the tool-help resource. `initialize` also returns MCP `instructions` for the first time (only `server/discover` carried a single generic sentence): a short intent-to-tool routing guide (`genexus_whoami` first, `genexus_query`/`genexus_search_source` to find, `genexus_kb action=open` before `select`, `genexus_read` of the edited part for its `versionToken`, `genexus_analyze mode=context`/`genexus_impact` before changing, `genexus_edit` with `dryRun` first, `genexus_lifecycle` polling with `target=op:<id>`). Each route is emitted only when the active tool profile exposes its tools, so the guide never points at a tool the client cannot call.

- `docs/README.md` indexes every document under `docs/` by area and is linked from the README and `AGENTS.md`, so feature guides such as the K2B Designer bridge and WorkWithPlus on Empty WebPanels are discoverable without reading the changelog. A CLI docs test fails when a new document is not listed or an index link is broken ([#450](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/450)).

- **Typed authoring for standard WorkWith pattern actions** ([#455](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/455)):
  - Exposes dedicated tool `genexus_workwith` (actions: `list_actions`, `add_action`), strictly isolated from WorkWithPlus (`genexus_wwp`), to inspect tabs and author actions with procedure calls, conditions, and parameters on standard WorkWith instances.
  - Native reflection adapter `WorkWithSdkAdapter` using `Artech.Patterns.WorkWith.WorkWithInstance` when hosted on the GeneXus STA thread, with robust XML parsing/mutation fallback for headless or test environments.
  - Fail-closed validation for missing targets, invalid action names, non-existent or non-Procedure targets, tab not found, duplicate action names, and pattern mismatches (with structured hints pointing to `genexus_wwp`).

### Changed

- Bumped MCP tool schema token budget (38,000 -> 38,500; measured 38,041), 'all' tool profile byte budget (100,000 -> 102,000; measured 100,907 bytes), and 'authoring' tool profile byte budget (68,500 -> 70,500; measured 69,808 bytes) to accommodate the new `genexus_workwith` tool definition and its actions ([#455](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/455)).
- Bumped MCP tool schema token budget (37,000 -> 38,000; measured 37,302), 'all' tool profile byte budget (89,000 -> 90,500; measured 89,144 bytes), and 'authoring' tool profile byte budget (62,500 -> 63,000; measured 62,520 bytes) to accommodate the new `genexus_wire` tool definition, its 10 actions, and cross-tool discovery guidance.

### Fixed

- **Pattern diagnostic and authoring boundary for WorkWith targets** ([#454](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/454)):
  - `PatternAnalysisService.ReadPatternPartXml` now verifies that a direct `PatternInstance` part matches the caller's requested `patternId` before extracting XML, returning a `PatternMismatch` diagnostic instead of exposing standard WorkWith instances to WorkWithPlus-specific callers.
  - `genexus_wwp` and `genexus_apply_pattern(mode=actions)` now reject standard WorkWith instances with `WWPInstanceNotFound` and actionable diagnostics (`detectedPatterns: [{"name": ..., "pattern": "WorkWith"}]`) instead of returning `WwpActionsRead` with an empty catalog or misleading `FormActionContainerNotFound` errors.
  - `PatchService` and `WritePatternPart` now emit pattern-aware hints when rejecting structural pattern edits (`PatternStructureChangeUnsupported`), clarifying that non-WWP patterns require the GeneXus IDE or a pattern-specific typed authoring action, rather than pointing to `genexus_wwp action=add_user_action`.

- **Pattern help accuracy, environment regeneration notes, and patch receipt delivery** ([#453](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/453), [#452](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/452)):
  - Updated `ToolHelpCatalog` to clarify that Selection ordering is not exposed as a typed MCP action and that raw `PatternInstance` edits reject `childrenOrderedList` as `PatternMetadataChangeUnsupported`, advising agents to configure Selection ordering in the GeneXus IDE.
  - Delimited recorded regeneration provenance between GeneXus builds (GX18 U10 regenerated derived objects, whereas on GX18 U15 and GX17 U4 headless instance saves persist without updating derived panels), advising IDE apply when derived objects do not follow.
  - `PatchService` now unpacks `writePayload["result"]` into `resultObj` so that `derivedObjectRegeneration`, `warning`, `resolvedObject`, `resolvedType`, and related diagnostic evidence are delivered directly at the top level of the MCP patch response.

- Remediated AI plugin scanner false positive findings and configured repository trust policy in the HOL Plugin Scanner workflow ([#451](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/451)).

### Internal

- Bumped GitHub Actions workflows and Nexus IDE development dependencies:
  - Updated `actions/checkout` (v7.0.1), `actions/setup-node` (v7.1.0), `actions/setup-dotnet` (v6.0.0), `actions/upload-artifact` (v7.0.2), and `ai-plugin-scanner-action` (v1.2.777) across CI, release, live-smoke, and scanner workflows ([#461](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/461), [#462](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/462), [#463](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/463), [#464](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/464), [#465](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/465)).
  - Bumped Nexus IDE devDependencies: `@vscode/test-electron` to 3.1.0, `mermaid` to 12.1.0, `@vscode/vsce` to 4.0.0, and `mocha` to 12.0.3 ([#468](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/468), [#470](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/470), [#471](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/471), [#472](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/472)).

## v3.13.0 - 2026-10-09


### Tracked issues

- [#441](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/441) — [Feature] WorkWithPlus on WebPanel: no way to add buttons (userAction / actionGroup / table) to an Empty-template instance
- [#442](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/442) — [Feature] WorkWithPlus on WebPanel: no way to add a variable grid (add_grid needs an SDT collection; grid leaf rejected)
- [#447](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/447) — [Integridade] Escrita textual de Variables reprocessa declarações fora do delta solicitado
- [#448](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/448) — [Integridade] Recibos de Variables podem confundir confirmação textual com ausência de mutação ou restauração integral
- [#449](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/449) — [Integridade] genexus_edit com parts[] perde dryRun no encaminhamento para BatchEdit


### Added

- An opt-in IDE bridge inspects and edits already-active K2B WebPanel Designers through the native GeneXus document path, with explicit KB/pipe selection, preview and version checks. It does not activate inactive Designers or convert legacy HWs. Thanks to @ip-chb ([#313](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/313), [#320](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/320)).

- **WorkWithPlus variable grids.** `genexus_wwp action=add_grid` now accepts ordered, typed variable columns without an SDT collection. Set `gridName`, `containerName` and `columns` to create a grid loaded by the panel's own events, including editable columns. [#442](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/442). Thanks to @davidagostini ([#445](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/445)).

### Changed

- Automatic Variables recovery no longer replayed text-only snapshots that cannot certify SDK metadata or an atomic conditional restore. Receipts now distinguished DSL confirmation from metadata verification and retained write/snapshot evidence when persisted text was unchanged. Typed `genexus_variable` operations and Source/Rules auto-declaration remained available ([#448](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/448); [verification and recovery limits](docs/variables-text-integrity.md)).

### Fixed

- Multipart `genexus_edit dryRun=true` reached the dispatcher and every full/patch item; item overrides could not enter the direct transaction or save path ([#449](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/449)).
- Textual Variables writes validated all declarations and referenced types before mutation, skipped setters/binders on unchanged declarations, and skipped Save for identical DSL. Multipart and target batches preflighted Variables before another item was written ([#447](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/447)).
- External Object method authoring applied native types, dimensions, parameter directions and external names, with previews, duplicate protection and independent post-save comparison. Reads exposed the full method contract. XPZ import captured SDK diagnostics and returned an error when ImportFile declined. Thanks to @davidagostini ([#446](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/446)).
- SDT and External Object failure recovery required an independent current owner and the version recorded after this operation's Save. Missing or changed ownership evidence refused recovery instead of re-saving a stale owner over concurrent edits.
- External Object reads and XPZ diagnostics no longer required SDK members absent from GeneXus 16. Generic-type reads exposed native availability rather than treating an unavailable SDK collection as confirmed empty.

- `genexus_properties action=get,reconcileTimedOutWrite=true` now performs an authoritative typed read of persisted values and clears a matching timeout fence only when every affected property and a version token are present. On WebPanels, `MainProgram` maps explicitly to the native `IsMain` property. Property writes support `dryRun`, `expectedVersion`, independent post-save verification and complete-object rollback on failure; WebPanel reference properties work in batches. No lifecycle operation runs implicitly. Thanks to @davidagostini ([#443](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/443)).

- `genexus_edit` and `genexus_delete_object` no longer wait behind whole-KB scans launched by the Worker's automatic warmup. SDK commands queued behind another long operation now receive `WorkerBusy` when their bounded wait expires. A Variables edit preview reports the resolved object, version and proposed content without saving. Full edits honor `expectedVersion` even when the Gateway also sends an empty `baseVersion`. GeneXus CopyModel write locks return `KbWriteLocked` with persisted-state evidence instead of a generic transaction failure. Thanks to @davidagostini ([#443](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/443)).

- SDT `update_visual` now previews every member omitted by replacement, requires explicit removal confirmation, and supports `mode=add` while preserving existing members. SDT revision history can read and restore `SDTStructure` with an optimistic version check, preview, native rollback source, and post-save verification. Thanks to @davidagostini ([#444](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/444)).

- **WorkWithPlus buttons on Empty WebPanels.** `add_user_action` now creates a missing `TableActions` under the unique `TableMain`, preserving existing content and deriving the button event from its name. Explicit missing containers and ambiguous parents remain errors. [#441](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/441). Thanks to @davidagostini ([#445](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/445)).

### Internal

- The combined PR #320 and #443–446 contract adds the opt-in K2B Designer tool and typed property, SDT, WorkWithPlus and External Object fields. The intentionally expanded schema budgets are 37,000 approximate tokens, 89,000 all-profile bytes and 62,500 authoring-profile bytes (measured 36,759 / 88,148 / 61,238 respectively), retaining existing guidance rather than removing older contracts to fit the former limits.

## v3.12.0 - 2026-10-06


### Tracked issues

- [#407](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/407) — [Bug] RPT_TEXT_MODE is neither readable nor writable, so GX8 text-mode reports fail to compile with CS0103
- [#437](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/437) — [Bug] failureTrace retorna vazio quando os recursos de UI do .NET Framework estão localizados (prefixo "em " em vez de "at ")
- [#438](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/438) — [Higiene] Linha com apenas espaços em BuildDiagnosticRecognitionTests.cs:34 é reportada por git diff --check
- [#439](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/439) — [Investigação] genexus_read devolve conteúdo anterior após Save pela IDE em leituras incondicionais


### Added

- `genexus_read refresh=true` bypasses Gateway/Worker response caches, invalidates the SDK object and independently resolves the same identity after an external IDE save. Ordinary reads now report Worker cache origin and observation age; refresh fails with `FreshReadUnavailable` if an independent read cannot be confirmed. This is not an atomic snapshot against concurrent IDE saves ([#439](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/439)).

### Fixed

- `genexus_properties` now reads and writes report Layout `RPT_TEXT_MODE`, also projected on the Report XML root. `True`/`False` writes require independent persisted read-back; unconfirmed writes fail closed. Character-coordinate conversion remains a separate enhancement ([#407](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/407)).

- `genexus_layout action=add_report_control` (and `move_report_control`) no longer report `ReportControlWriteVerificationFailed` for controls the SDK persisted exactly as requested: geometry is now verified against the `Left`/`Top` projection of the SDK read-back instead of `X`/`Y`, and `font`/`fontName`/`fontSize` against the read-back `FontName`/`FontSize`.

- `kind=variable` now creates the control GeneXus really uses for a variable, a `ReportAttribute` bound through `AttributeReference` (`&Name`); the `ReportVariable` type does not exist in the SDK, so the control used to be cloned from another type and left unbound. `controlType=ReportVariable` is accepted as an alias. GeneXus names such a control after its reference and ignores any other name, so it is created and verified as `&Name`; the response keeps the requested name in `requestedControlName`. The requested control type is also no longer replaced by a clone of whatever control the print block already had.

- `fontName`/`fontSize` are now applied to report controls (the SDK exposes a single `Font` object, which is rebuilt from the current one) and projected back as `FontName`/`FontSize`.

- A report-control verification failure with `rollbackOnFailure=true` now actually restores the original layout and reports `rolledBack:true`: the rollback fence is checked against the state read right after the write (not the candidate XML, which the SDK always normalizes), and the restore uses that state as its baseline so controls added by the failed write are removed.

- `genexus_layout set_property` / `set_properties` with `propertyName=Font` on a Procedure report control no longer fails with `LayoutWriteVerificationFailed` and a rollback. The Font is a `System.Drawing.Font`: the writer had no conversion for it and the read-back (`Font.ToString()`) never string-matched the request. It now accepts the `get_tree` form (`[Font: Name=Arial, Size=12, Units=3, ...]`) and the invariant `Arial, 12pt[, style=Bold]` form, and verifies by name, size and units (style when both sides state one).

- `genexus_layout` `add_printblock` / `rename_printblock` / `delete_printblock` silently edited the Procedure Source (add inserted `print <name>`) without saying so. The response now reports `result.sourceChanged` and `result.sourceChange` (`line`, `removedLines`, `addedLines`), and `add_printblock` accepts `appendPrintToSource=false` (default `true`) to leave Source untouched. Documented in the tool help and schema.

- `genexus_read part=GroupStructure` on a Group returned only the `IsDefault` property XML; it now returns one `Subtype -> Supertype` line per member, and a Group read without a part defaults to that part.

- `genexus_run_object` no longer returns a made-up `http://localhost/portal3_desenv/...` URL. The base URL is now the `baseUrl` the user set in `preview.config.json`, else the active environment's Web Root (the generator property `WebRoot`, read from the main generator of the target model; a full URL is used as-is, a bare virtual directory is served from `http://localhost/`); the auto-generated legacy default is ignored. When neither exists it returns `BaseUrlUnresolved` with a hint. The response reports `baseUrlSource` (`config` | `environment`).

- Spec-checks of never-built objects no longer blocked the Worker, and typed Transaction targets retained their type when a Table shared the name. Thanks to @danielkrueger ([#403](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/403)).

- Write-failure traces retained method names with localized .NET UI resources, including Portuguese, without exposing source paths or arguments ([#437](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/437)).

- `genexus_edit validationMode="specify"` now returns the itemized diagnostics (raw line, code, object, line) in the `SpecificationFailed` error instead of only a count, because the Gateway terse error projection dropped them. The edit response (error and `_meta.specification`) also carries the specify pass warnings and `staleEnvironmentCopy: true` when GeneXus specified the environment copy that changed since the last build. Thanks to @danielkrueger ([#428](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/pull/428)).

- `genexus_edit` full-mode writes of an XML part (Structure, WebForm) with `verifyMode` omitted no longer fail with `WriteNotPersisted` / `reason: lineEndings` when only the line endings differ from the SDK re-read (the SDK always stores CRLF). The gateway forwards an omitted `verifyMode` as a blank string, which was resolved to a strict `exact` check for XML parts; blank now means omitted (`normalized`). An explicit `verifyMode: "exact"` is unchanged and genuine content differences still report `contentMismatch`.

- `genexus_lifecycle action=build dryRun=true` now reports how the build plan was expanded instead of only `wouldBuild`: `seeds`, `callees` (callees and `_bc` variants the real build adds ahead of the targets), `seedCount`, `calleeCount`, `totalCount`, `truncated`, `indexLoaded` and `callerGraphAvailable`. When callee expansion is requested but the index is not loaded, the caller graph is missing, or no callees were found, the preview adds a `warnings` entry instead of silently listing only the requested target. `includeCallees` still defaults to `transitive`.

- **Build evidence lists every generated file of the object, not just the freshest one.** `generateEvidence.filesWritten[]` (and `upToDate[]`) now carry a `files[]` array with every generated `.cs/.aspx/.js/.html` of the object, including the `a<name>` Main-object variant, plus `bin\<name>.dll` and `bin\a<name>.dll`, each with `lastWriteUtc` and `writtenDuringBuild` (last write at or after the build start). A Main procedure with Call protocol HTTP builds `<name>.cs` (stub), `a<name>.cs` (the real program) and `bin\a<name>.dll`, but the evidence used to name only the single freshest file. The `genexus_lifecycle` help now states that `generateEvidence` is an output of the terminal build result (there is no `generateEvidence` input parameter, and it is absent from the immediate queued/running reply).

### Internal

- Added review tooling that failed in practice: `scripts/run-gate.ps1` runs long gates detached with a single bounded `Wait` (passed/failed/running/died), `scripts/merge-tool-definitions.py` three-way merges `tool_definitions.json` by tool and field, review worktrees link the main `node_modules` when lockfiles match, and the integration preflight stops at once when `node_modules` is missing.

- Cleared every compiler and analyzer warning in the solution (nullable annotations and flow fixes in the Gateway, nullable and xUnit analyzer fixes in both test projects), with no suppressions and no assertion weakened; the Release warning baseline is now empty (0 locations).

- Fixed fork-PR submission on the installed GitHub CLI by deriving the base repository from the verified GitHub PR URL instead of requesting an unsupported JSON field; clean-tree, fresh-base, preflight and explicit-ref guards remained enforced.

- Removed trailing whitespace from the build-diagnostic regression fixture ([#438](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/438)).

## v3.11.1 - 2026-10-05


### Tracked issues

- [#351](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/351) — [Feature] Há caminho SDK para acrescentar filtros de seleção ao WorkWith padrão no GeneXus 18?
- [#361](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/361) — [Bug] Report (Procedure) Layout: import_part leaves variable-bound controls "(none)", re-import duplicates them, PrintBlock Height is ignored; add_report_control / set_property cannot fix it
- [#367](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/367) — [Bug] Capacity eviction can still stop a Worker running an async build or a timed-out command
- [#372](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/372) — [Perf] Worker memory budgets for large KBs are still missing (second half of #344)
- [#375](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/375) — [Reliability] A valid shared Worker response above 16 MiB still stops the broker for every client (deferred part of #347)
- [#385](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/385) — [Feature/Bug] Lacunas que obrigaram trabalho manual na IDE: Query, coleção, save_as, modo texto de relatório, WWP, diagnósticos de build
- [#404](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/404) — [Feature] A Query object's structure cannot be read or written (parameters/query elements/output formats)
- [#406](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/406) — [Bug] save_as fails cloning part 'Layout' for a report Procedure (regression from the #118 hardening)
- [#408](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/408) — [Feature] WorkWithPlus/PatternInstance: a new control element (combo, query viewer) cannot be added via import_part
- [#411](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/411) — [Enhancement] A build does not warn that an internal callee's generated .cs is stale (deferred item from #409)
- [#414](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/414) — [Bug] User action gxobject is written as <object GUID>-<bare name>; the IDE stores <type GUID>-<qualified name>
- [#415](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/415) — [Bug] add_user_action only finds TableActions or a table that already holds an action, and TableActions is always ambiguous on a transaction WWP
- [#416](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/416) — [Bug] genexus_versioning undo reports UndoFailed for a part it restored
- [#417](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/417) — [Bug] Variables DSL drops decimals: NUMERIC(19,2) is written as NUMERIC(19)
- [#418](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/418) — [Bug] A build the Worker already finished can keep the lifecycle FIFO blocked; later builds stay queued indefinitely
- [#419](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/419) — [Bug] A failed native WorkWithPlus write reports only "Exception has been thrown by the target of an invocation"
- [#420](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/420) — [Feature] genexus_wwp: add a grid over an SDT collection to a WorkWithPlus instance (with the typed attributes it needs)
- [#421](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/421) — [Feature] genexus_wwp add_user_action: a button that opens an object (callObject, popup, parameters)
- [#422](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/422) — [Feature] genexus_dfd: entity-relationship diagram of a Transaction/Table from the SDK's own table relations
- [#423](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/423) — [Feature] genexus_impact: affected set with a dependencies-first rebuild order and cycle detection, instead of a heuristic risk score
- [#424](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/424) — [Feature] genexus_object_context: one object's identity, references and data model in a single call
- [#425](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/425) — [Feature] genexus_structure: read and set "Show in Default Forms" per Transaction attribute
- [#426](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/426) — [Feature] genexus_wwp list_tabs / tab_schema: what a WWP instance has and what the pattern allows, before add_tab
- [#427](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/427) — [Feature] genexus_security audit_object: fast, in-memory OWASP surface audit of a single object


### Added

- `genexus_read` / `genexus_edit part=QueryStructure` (or the default `Source`) now read and write a Query object's structure as text: one `[Elements]`, `[Parameters]`, `[Filters]` and `[Orders]` section, one SDK-serialized item per line, through the part's own `ToSerializedStrings`/`FromSerializedStrings`. A write is saved on the part itself and reported only when the re-read has the same item counts. `export_kb_to_text format=native` carries the same text. Output formats are not covered. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/404
- A WorkWithPlus/PatternInstance `import_part` can now insert a `<data attribute="&Var" ...>` leaf element (the form PopupLayoutBuilder emits for a combo, radio or edit control bound to a variable, with `PATTERN_ELEMENT_CUSTOM_PROPERTIES`). Children and attributes outside its list are still refused. Query viewer and SDT grid elements, and exposing `patternEditMode`, are not covered: they cannot be verified without the WorkWithPlus package. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/408
- `genexus_wwp action=add_grid` writes a grid over an SDT collection variable the way WorkWithPlus's own wizard does: `collection` (`&Lines`), `sdt`, `containerName` (a table), `columns` (item names or `{item, description}`) and an optional `deleteAction` (a `UDelete` image action). Every column is a `gridVariable` named after the collection and bound to an SDT item through `sdtItem`, with `domain = <SDT type GUID>-<Module.Sdt>`. The native write sets the typed attributes with their types (`domain` as the SDT `KBObject`, `cellThemeClass`/`imageClass` as `DropDownValueOptionCustomType`), shares the form-action path (snapshots, save, re-read of every column in order, projection, rollback) and fails when the parent WebForm is unchanged after projection. Refused: a missing or invalid collection, SDT, container or columns, a repeated item, an item that is not in the SDT, an action group as container, and a second grid over the same collection. A typed `page` parameter and generating `&UDelete.Click` are not covered, and the native write was not exercised against a live WorkWithPlus instance. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/420
- `genexus_wwp action=add_user_action` accepts `callObject` (`Name` or `Type:Name`), `popup` and ordered `parameters`: the button opens another object and has no derived `Do<actionName>` event. `gxobject` is written as the object itself (`<type GUID>-<qualified name>`) and `popup` as a boolean, and the post-save re-read rejects a lost object, a lost popup flag or reordered parameters; `popup`/`parameters` without `callObject`, or `callObject` together with `procedure`, are refused. The native write with `callObject` was not exercised against a live WorkWithPlus instance. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/421
- `genexus_dfd` (new, read-only) returns the entity-relationship diagram of a Transaction or Table, built only from the table relations the SDK reports (`extends` for the extended table, `subordinates` for subordinated tables), as `tables`, `edges`, a Mermaid `erDiagram` and `stats` naming what truncated it (`maxDepth`, default 3; `maxTables`). A Transaction without an associated table gets `NoAssociatedTable`, not an empty diagram. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/422
- `genexus_impact` (new, read-only) returns the affected set of an object over the cached caller/callee graph: depth per object, `countsByType`, a dependencies-first rebuild `order` (an object comes after everything it calls), `hasCycle`/`cycleNodes`, `stats.truncatedBy` naming the cap that was hit (`maxDepth` 1-6, `maxNodes` 1-2000), `typeFilter` (narrows the report, not the traversal) and `fieldTrust` (`partial:<pct>` means part of the graph was not enriched, so an empty set is not a confirmed zero). There is no risk score. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/423
- `genexus_object_context` (new, read-only) returns one object's identity (from the index, no SDK open), callers/callees with coverage (`read:false` when empty under partial coverage) and data model (the ER diagram for a Transaction or Table, the index tables otherwise, or the reason it was left out in `suppressed[]`). A name shared by several objects returns `AmbiguousName` with each guid and path; `guid`, `entityKey` and `path` (with or without the `Root Module/` prefix) select one and never fall back to a homonym. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/424
- `genexus_structure get_visual`/`update_visual` report and set `showInDefaultForms` per Transaction attribute (the Structure row's "Show in Default Forms", `IncludeInForms`) through the SDK helpers by reflection. `update_visual` writes it only when it differs, rejects non-booleans and fails when the SDK cannot write it; the dry-run diff and the post-save read-back include it. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/425
- `genexus_wwp action=list_tabs` reports every tab of an instance (`gridTab`, `tabularTab`, `webComponentTab`) with its kind, position and key attributes, plus a `conventions` block with only the attributes all tabs of a kind share. `action=tab_schema kind=grid|tabular|webcomponent` reads the pattern's own element definition from a tab of that kind in the instance; a kind the instance lacks, or a package that does not expose the definition, is a typed error (`WwpTabKindNotInInstance`, `WwpPatternSchemaUnavailable`), never an empty schema. The WorkWithPlus package is not installed where this was developed, so `tab_schema` is not verified against a real package. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/426
- `genexus_security action=audit_object` is a fast in-memory audit of one object against the IDE Security Scanner rules whose definition is known here: #100 (URL parameters not encrypted: always on a Transaction or WebPanel, on a Procedure only when `CALL_PROTOCOL` is HTTP) and #108 (native `csharp`/`java` blocks). `rulesNotEvaluated` lists the IDE rules it does not run (#105, #139) and it complements `scan_native`. The IDE's Checks.xml is not available in a headless install, so the rule set, the applicability conditions and the `warning` severity are not verified against the IDE, and the audit was not compared with a live `scan_native` run. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/427

### Changed

- Capacity eviction no longer stops a Worker that is still running work after its RPC returned. A queued or running lifecycle/background job on the Worker's KB, or a pending request routed to it (kept after a client timeout), now makes the entry ineligible; a stalled job does not pin it. The command reservation is taken against the Worker the pool still owns, so an eviction between acquire and reserve is retried instead of routing the command to a stopped process. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/367
- The response caches of `genexus_list_objects`, `genexus_query` and the summary are bounded by estimated UTF-16 bytes (32 MiB, 32 MiB, 16 MiB) as well as by entry count, and `genexus_doctor` reports the worker's private memory and each cache's estimated bytes. Promoting a source into the in-memory FullSource is also skipped while the Worker's private memory is above 1400 MiB (the source stays in the persistent source store); the character budgets themselves are unchanged. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/372
- A shared Worker response above the 16 MiB hard ceiling no longer stops the broker for every attachment when its request id can be recovered. The broker reads the child's stdout with a bounded line reader that keeps at most the ceiling and discards the rest, answers the owning request with `WorkerResponseTooLarge`, and stays fail-closed for frames whose owner it cannot establish. The Worker now writes `id` before `result` in every response so the id is in the retained prefix. In a shared child, a response above the 4 MiB frame bound is replaced by the same `WorkerResponseTooLarge` error (with the measured size) before it is written, so it never crosses the pipe. Per-route paging of large read/batch results is not part of this change. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/375
- A build now warns (`[stale-callee]`, in `warnings`, never in `errorCount`) when a callee outside the build has a generated `.cs` older than the callee's last edit. A callee with no generated file stays with `[generate-gap]`. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/411

### Fixed

- `genexus_create action=save_as` of a report Procedure copies the `Layout` part natively and keeps its controls. The textual path either aborted with `SaveAsPartFailed` or, for new controls, dropped them without an error; the copy is now accepted only when its layout projection equals the source's, otherwise the part fails with `LayoutCloneNotFaithful`. The clone write also passes the source `typeFilter`. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/406
- Report Layout writes (`genexus_edit`/`import_part part=Layout`, `genexus_layout set_property`) now persist a print block's `Height`; bind a control to a new `&variable` or an attribute (the SDK reference is resolved to the Variable/Attribute instead of `(none)`); and no longer duplicate or delete bound controls on re-import, because a control bound to a reference is named after it and is now matched by that reference. The `add_report_control` overlap message now names an unnamed unbound control instead of `''`. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/361
- A same-named Folder, Table or other non-parent object no longer becomes the parent of a WorkWithPlus instance: the parent is resolved by type (Transaction, then WebPanel), its name is read from the instance's `<type GUID>-<Name>` `transaction` attribute before the `WorkWithPlus<X>` convention, and the rollback restores the WebForm of the parent's own type. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/413
- A user action's `gxobject` is written as `<type GUID>-<qualified name>`, as the IDE does, instead of the object's own GUID and bare name. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/414
- Form-action containers can be any table or action group (an empty `TableContent`, a root action group), and a name repeated across tabs is selected with a path of named ancestors such as `General/Table/Actions`. `FormActionContainerAmbiguous` lists each candidate's `path`, `FormActionContainerNotFound` lists `availablePaths`, and a native action group is recognised by its type or its XML element name. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/415
- `genexus_versioning` undo no longer reports `UndoFailed` for a part it restored: a write answering `status: ok` with a `message`, or with an explicit null `error`, is a success. The canonical status decides first; `error`/`message` presence decides only when there is no status. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/416
- Variables written through the text path keep the decimals of numeric types: `NUMERIC(19,2)` is no longer stored as `NUMERIC(19)`, and re-declaring `NUMERIC(19)` clears a previous `,2`. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/417
- A build the Worker already finished no longer keeps later builds queued: before admitting a serialized lifecycle job the Gateway reconciles the scope's running jobs that hold a Worker task id against the Worker, and the next queued job is promoted once one is confirmed finished. Why the poller sometimes does not complete its job was not established. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/418
- A failed native WorkWithPlus write reports the real cause instead of `Exception has been thrown by the target of an invocation`: reflection and aggregate wrappers are unwrapped to the root exception, the response carries `exceptionType` and `failureTrace` (method names only), the Worker logs the same line, and the Gateway's terse error projection keeps both fields. https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/419

### Internal

- Issue bookkeeping, no code change: https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/351 was answered in the v3.11.0 notes (a typed SDK route exists), and https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/385 was an umbrella report split into per-gap issues (#404-#410) that were fixed individually.

## v3.11.0 - 2026-10-04


### Tracked issues

- [#362](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/362) — [Bug] Shared WorkerHost supervision reports the broker's own state instead of the child's, and never raises a recycle election
- [#363](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/363) — [Bug] Source-store eviction never subtracts evicted bytes, so every later insert evicts more of the store
- [#364](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/364) — [Perf] FullSource promotion still sums every indexed source length on each promotion
- [#365](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/365) — [Bug] First-touch warm scope key ignores the KB being warmed, so a second KB is still suppressed
- [#366](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/366) — [Bug] Caller-site continuation is unreachable from the public tool, and a single oversized caller stalls paging forever
- [#368](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/368) — [Bug] inspect still reads live SDK objects on Task.Run and late sections still mutate the returned response
- [#369](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/369) — [Bug] STA admission byte budget ignores the payload, has no per-client bound, and the Gateway channel is still unbounded
- [#370](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/370) — [Bug] Log grep/since/correlation filters now only search the last N lines instead of the whole log
- [#371](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/371) — [Bug] SDK stall detection never fires for a call that has not emitted progress
- [#373](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/373) — [Perf] Lite-walk checkpoints still serialize the whole accumulated snapshot (second half of #345)
- [#374](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/374) — [Perf] Warm reopen still reads and decompresses every stored body to rebuild trigram postings (deferred part of #339)
- [#376](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/376) — [Bug] Conditional reads can return notModified from the Gateway semantic cache without the Worker checking freshness
- [#377](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/377) — [Bug] Multi-KB discovery loses results on a budget cut and reports paged KBs as complete
- [#378](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/378) — [Bug] LiteEntryAccumulator duplicate-GUID fallback leaves stale slots and can tombstone the wrong entry
- [#379](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/379) — [Bug] A resource-updated notification without a KB alias clears the semantic cache of every KB
- [#390](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/390) — Flaky test: A_Corrupted_Body_On_Disk_Is_Not_Reported_As_Certified (mtime set within the same clock tick)
- [#405](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/405) — [Bug] A variable of SDT, BC or built-in type cannot be marked as a collection: modify reports 'Domain ... was not found'
- [#409](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/409) — [Bug] Build diagnostics: dropped severity loses compiler errors, and the [generate-gap] warning is false for Main procedures
- [#410](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/410) — [Bug] genexus_edit part=Variables mode=patch reports WriteNotPersisted when the lines were written at another index
- [#412](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/412) — [Bug] release-preflight.ps1 crashes in Add-PreflightPhaseResult, aborting the release after the long phases

### Added

### Changed

- The Worker's SDK admission budget now bounds the memory it was asked to bound, and no single client can monopolise it. `StaScheduler.EstimateBytes` charged only the `id`/`method`/`action` strings - a few dozen bytes - while the queued item also retained `RawLine`, the whole command text including any edit or import payload. A queued 10 MiB edit was therefore charged about a hundred bytes, `MaxQueuedBytes` (32 MiB) could not be reached before `MaxQueuedCommands` (512) did, and 512 accepted large commands retained hundreds of megabytes inside a 32-bit Worker: the count budget was the only thing that ever stopped a burst, and the byte budget was decorative. The charge now covers the raw line, the header strings and a fixed per-item overhead, and each item stores its charge at admission so dequeue, timeout and cancellation all refund exactly what was charged. Admission additionally carries per-client caps (half of each global budget), because a global-only budget is a denial-of-service primitive: one client flooding P2 work used to exhaust every slot and have every *other* client's interactive read refused with `WorkerQueueSaturated`, and per-client fairness at dequeue time could not help because the work was never admitted. The per-client caps deliberately apply to background and normal work only, which is the reserved headroom the issue asks for: interactive (P0) reads stay bounded by the global budgets alone, so a client's own background flood cannot lock that same client out of its own reads. A per-client refusal is reported as `client_command_cap` / `client_bytes_exhausted` rather than as a saturated queue ([#369](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/369)).

### Fixed

- Release preflight no longer aborts after its long phases when a non-phase value reaches the summary: `Add-PreflightPhaseResult` rejects a result without `name`/`status`, ignores foreign elements when merging, and the phase completion returns only the phase object. [#412](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/412)

- A build no longer under-reports its own diagnostics. `BuildService.HandleLine` receives an `isError` flag and never read it: severity was re-derived from text, and both `_rxError` and `_rxSpecError` require a literal `error`. The in-process engine routes *every* message through `LogMessageEvent`, which passes `isError: false` and cannot tell a diagnostic from progress chatter, so the severity the BL engine knows was discarded on the way in. A `spc0031` line was therefore pushed into `TailLines` and never counted, so `specify` returned `errorCount 0` for a run that had failed and the caller saw only tail lines - and the same loss is what produced `NativeBuildFailed ... compiler diagnostics were not captured` on the `includeCallees=none` route, which is what hid a real `CS0246`. A GeneXus spec/gen diagnostic at the start of a line is now recognized in its bare form (`spc`/`gen`/`src`/`qry` plus four digits). It is anchored at the line start so ordinary prose cannot match, and `spc0217` is deliberately excluded because the build evidence already treats "unreachable" as an expected notice rather than a gap - counting it would turn a passing build into a failing one ([#409](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/409)).
- The `[generate-gap]` warning is no longer raised falsely for every Main report build. The freshness probe globbed `target + ".*"` and then required an exact filename match, but the .NET generator prefixes a Main object's source with `a`, so `rcliser2sql` exists only as `arcliser2sql.cs`. The probe could not see the file, reported it missing, and every build of a Main procedure warned. The prefixed spelling is probed too; both spellings are still matched exactly and the extension must still be one we diff, so a similarly-named sibling is never mistaken for the target ([#409](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/409)).
- A variable of SDT, Business Component or built-in type can be marked as a collection. Two separate refusals combined to make it impossible. `genexus_variable action=modify collection=true` demanded a `newTypeName`, because `collection` was grouped with the arguments that reshape a type; and supplying one did not help either, because `VariableTypeResolver` classifies *every* bare non-primitive name as `DomainReference` - SDT, BC and every built-in GeneXus type included - while the modify path resolved only a Domain and answered `Domain 'SDTDashRec' was not found` before the binding code that handles those types could ever run. `collection` is a flag on the variable's existing type rather than a retype, so a collection-only modify no longer needs a type name and keeps the current type; and a retype now refuses only when *nothing* can resolve the name, falling through to the ordered resolution `add` already uses (Domain, then SDT/Business Component, then the two built-in registries). That decision is made by a new side-effect-free `VariableInjector.IsGenexusDataType` probe, because it has to run before there is a variable to bind onto, and it mirrors `TryBindGenexusDataType`'s lookup and its Domain refusal exactly so the two cannot disagree about what is bindable. The collection flag is verified on read-back and the rollback restores both flags it touched, and an unknown type name still returns `UnknownType` ([#405](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/405)).
- A `Variables` patch that succeeded is no longer reported as `WriteNotPersisted`. `genexus_edit part=Variables mode=patch`, adding declarations, answered `WriteNotPersisted` while a re-read showed the lines present - only at a different position, which is where the SDK puts a newly declared variable. The write was always fine; the verification disagreed. Every persistence comparator is positional: `normalized` is a verbatim string compare of the normalized text, and both `NormalizedCodeEquals` and `ModuleQualificationEquals` bail on a length change and then compare index-wise, so a reordering fails all of them. Verification now also accepts a `Variables` part whose two sides hold the same multiset of normalized lines. It is deliberately scoped to `Variables`, and deliberately a multiset rather than a containment check: declaration order is not content there, whereas rule order in a `Source` part is, and this file's header records that a false "verified" masks a real content divergence. A patch that failed to apply a requested line, or that dropped a pre-existing one, still reports a mismatch, and an explicit `verifyMode=exact` still answers positionally ([#410](https://github.com/lennix1337/Genexus18MCP/issues/410)).
- The Gateway's per-Worker command channel is bounded, so a burst is refused instead of buffered without limit. `WorkerProcess._commandChannel` was `Channel.CreateUnbounded`, so a client that out-pushed the Worker's pipe reader retained an arbitrary backlog in the Gateway *before* the Worker applied any admission control of its own.
